1b1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//rgya gar skad du/sad b+ha rmA rA dza ma hA yA na sU tra/bod skad du/dam pa'i chos kyi   
1b2(mdo sna tshogs): rgyal po theg pa chen po'i mdo//de'i tshe na sangs rgyas shing sa la gnyis kyi bar na bzhugs te/mya ngan las 
1b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'das par gshegs kar sems can thams cad brgya stong khri 'bum phal mang po kun shing gi drung na sangs rgyas   
1b4(mdo sna tshogs): 'khor bar bskor te 'khor nas mig ni yar bcom ldan 'das kyi zhal du lta ste/cung zad kyang mi g.yengs de   
1b5(mdo sna tshogs): nas yang dag par gshegs pas 'phrul gyi stobs kyis 'od chen po btang nas nam mkha'i khams thams cad   
1b6(mdo sna tshogs): khyab par snang ste/'od de'i nang nas chos kun mngon par bstan nas/de'i tshe mang po'i nang na byang chub sems   
1b7(mdo sna tshogs): dpa'i ming nam mkha' mdzod ces bya ba zhig yod bstan las lags te/sangs rgyas la lan gsum 
1b8(mdo sna tshogs): bskor nas slar dos gcig tu 'dug ste/lus smad lngas la gdugs te mchi ma blags nas sangs 
2a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//rgyas la gsol pa/lha'i nang na btsun pa yang dag par gshegs pas mya ngan las 'das par gshegs pa'i dus kyang nye/gshegs su yang sla na/gshegs pa'i slad na rnyog pa 
2a2(mdo sna tshogs): rnam pa lnga'i sems can las ngan pa mang po bgyid cing ngan pa bcu sha stag spyod pa 'di lta bu'i sems can legs pa'i dpal gyi stobs snang ba la sangs rgyas kyis shin tu zab cing /dam pa'i chos kyi 
2a3(mdo sna tshogs): yi ge bcu gnyis bshad pa yi ge'i 'bru ni mangs/gzhung ni rgya ches pas khong du chud cing 'tshal bar bgyi ba'ang dka'/chos 'di kun blta zhing rjes kyang mi chod na/sangs rgyas kyis byams shing 
2a4(mdo sna tshogs): thugs rjes gzigs te/theg pa chen po'i chos yang dag pa'i bden pa bye brag phyed par bshad de/sems can rnams yang dag pa'i sman bzang po thob par bgyis te/nang gdug go cog sos par 
2a5(mdo sna tshogs): bgyir smon to//sangs rgyas kyis byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha'i mdzod la bka' stsal pa/shin tu dge'o shin tu dge'o//rigs kyi bu pho khyod kyis sems can rnams kyi phyir 'di lta bu'i don chen 
2a6(mdo sna tshogs): po 'dri nus pa bsod nams chen po thob ste/bsam gyis mi khyab po//ngas khyod la yang dag pa'i bden pa/theg pa chen po'i chos bye brag phyed pa/mi 'gyur ba'i gzhung rdzogs bshad do//ci'i phyir zhes 
2a7(mdo sna tshogs): byas na/sems can rgya cher thar par bya ba'i phyir ro//sems can rnams mya ngan dang bral bar bya ba'i phyir ro//semasacan dmyal ba'i nyon mongs pa las phyin cing yul gtsang mar skye bar bya ba'i phyir 
2a8(mdo sna tshogs): ro//yang dag par grol thar cing skye shi las 'das par bya ba'i phyir khyed rnams kun sems gcig tu soms shig dang khyed la go bar bshad do//de nas phal mang po kun shin tu dga' gzha' ste/kha tha dad 
2b1(mdo sna tshogs): kyis kyang tshig 'thun par gcig char smras pa/sangs rgyas kyis byams shing thugs rjes gzigs te/bdag cag rnams la bye brag phyed par bshad par smon te/sangs rgyas kyi zhal nas rigs kyi 
2b2(mdo sna tshogs): bu pho rnams grol thar par 'dod na/'dod pa'i yan lag bcad la sems gnyis su mi bsam par gcig tu bsam te yod pas sam pa'i sems btang ngo //sems kyi ngo bo nyid kyi tshul ni stong pa ste/
2b3(mdo sna tshogs): sems kyi ngo bo nyid kyi nang na 'go ba'ang med do//gtad pa'ang med do//blang ba dang btang ba med na ci'ang thob pa med do//de'i ming ni byang chub po//ji lta zhe na/mya ngan phal mang po kun ni sems gcig las 
2b4(mdo sna tshogs): skyes so//sems mi sems na mya ngan kyang mi skye ste/'du mched dang /shes rab rnams la blang ba dang gtang ba med do//blang ba dang gtang ba med pa 'dod chags so chog dang bral lo//'dod chags so chog 
2b5(mdo sna tshogs): dang bral na 'dod pa'i yan lag med do//byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha' mdzod kyis sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das sems can gyi 'du mched dang /shes rabakyi legs nyes skye nus 
2b6(mdo sna tshogs): shing /lan pon ldan pa'i gnas lags te/phyi nang mu gnyis kyi chos so chog gcig gi nang du gcig zhugs nas/ci'i slad nas de'i nang na blang ba dang btang ba ma mchis shes bgyid du/sangs rgyas kyis 
2b7(mdo sna tshogs): byang chub sems dpa' nam 'kha' mdzod la bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu pho bsam gtan gcig tu 'dus pa'i nang du zhugs la kun gyis phyi nang du brtags na tha mar yod med do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/nang du 
2b8(mdo sna tshogs): bltas na 'du brtags na yang dag pa'i ngo bo nyid la skye ba med/phyir bltas phyir brtags na/ma rig pa mi ldang /skye ba'ang med/ldang ba'ang med na/mya ngan las 'das pa'ang med de/de ni gtsang 
3a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//dang ba yin no//de ni mchog tu dga' ba'i sman yin no//byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha' mdzod kyis sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das yang dag par gshegs pas theg pa 
3a2(mdo sna tshogs): chen po'i chos yang dag pa'i gzugs shin tu zab cing gces pa/bla na ma mchis pa'i sman bzang po dang/theg pa gcig gi bden pa'i nang du 'jug pa bshad na/slan chad kyi sems can mi gtsang ba'i las gsum 
3a3(mdo sna tshogs): gyis ngan pa bcu'i las bgyis te/log sred kyi las spyad pas rtsa ba dang gzhi phra zhing zhan te//sems la gzungs su gzung ba 'ang dka' nas sman dang nad kyang gal te mi mthun na thabs ji ltar bgyis na theg pa chen po'i nang 
3a4(mdo sna tshogs): du 'jug par rung/sangs rgyas kyis byang chub sems dpa' namamakha' mdzod la bka' stsal pa/nga la thabs yod kyis theg pa chen po la 'jug par bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' ma mkha' mdzod kyis sangs rgas 
3a5(mdo sna tshogs): la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'a das bdag gis gnas sngon thos pa yang dag par gshegs pa sa theg pa chen po'i mi la ni pha rol tu phyin pa drug gi chos bshad/theg pa bar ma'i mi la ni rgyu rkyen bcu gnyis kyi chos bshad 
3a6(mdo sna tshogs): theg pa chung ngu'i mi la ni bden pa bzhi'i chos bshad/log sred kyi mi la ni dge ba bcu'i chos bshad de/kun kyang nad kyi gzugs dang sbyar nas chos kyi sman bzang po bshad na/de ci'i slad nas theg pa gcig gi 
3a7(mdo sna tshogs): chos bshad de/mi 'di'i rnam bzhi'i ra mda' bgyi/sangs rgyas kyis byang chubasems dpa' nam mkha' mdzod la bka' stsalapa/ngas theg pa gcig gi lam gyi chos bshad pa 'di ni dper nas gcig gis 
3a8(mdo sna tshogs): yod do cog bskyed cing thams cad gso nus pa dang mtshungs so//yang dper na chos kyi char ba'i rlan gyis thams cad la khyab par yog ste/sa las skye bo cog thams cad ngoms so//dper na 'bras sna cig gi chan 
3b1(mdo sna tshogs): bro ba la 'jig rten gyi sems can mi dang mi ma yin pa kun za'o//zos so chog kun lus ni skye/srog ni 'tsho ste/kun skye bar byas pa ni dper na sman gyi rgyal po sman sbyor mkhas pas sman ces pa bzang po kun sbyar te/sems 
3b2(mdo sna tshogs): can tsha ba'i nad kyis btab pa la btang na ni grang bsil sems can grang ba'i nad kyis btab pa gtang na ni dro tsha'o//tshad pa na ba la btang na ni tshad do//'khyil pa la btang na ni phol te nad ni sos pa med do//ngas 
3b3(mdo sna tshogs): theg pa gcig bshad de mi de'i gzhi'i nad rnams gso ba yang de dang mtshungs so//byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha' mdzod kyis sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das thabs ji lta bu spyad de/ngan pa bcu 
3b4(mdo sna tshogs): dang sems can log sred de theg pa gcig gcig gi lam du 'jug par bgyi/sangs rgyas kyis byang chub sems dpa' nam 'kha' mdzod la bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu pho nga'i theg pa gcig gi chos la 
3b5(mdo sna tshogs): theg pa gsum ldan te/yang gud na theg pa gsum zhes bya ba bshad pa med do//khyod brtags par nyon cig dang khyod la zhib du bshad do//rigs kyi bu pho nga dam pa'i chos kyi lam mo//phyi mo'i gzhi ngo bo nyid 
3b6(mdo sna tshogs): gcig ste/gnyis su med na thabs spyad pa'i phyir theg pa gsum du bshad do//theg pa gsum gyi chos kun gcig tu 'dus par brtag/sems can no cog la rnam pa bzhi yod mod kyi sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid la 
3b7(mdo sna tshogs): ni gnyis su med do//ji lta zhes byas na/sangs rgyas so cog dang /sems can no cog gi ngo bo nyid gcig tu 'thun te/mdo gcig ste tha dad pa med na/sems can rang gi sems kyis bye brag dbye ba g.yos so//yod 
3b8(mdo sna tshogs): ni sangs rgyas kyang sems can yin/sems can kyang sangs rgyas yin te/sems can no cog la yang sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid yod do//sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid dang /sems can gyi ngo bo nyid de gnyis kyang gcig 
4a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//
4b1(mdo sna tshogs): kyi bdag gi sems la rang langs te/de ni bdag gi rang bzhin gyi stobs so//rigs kyi bu pho de sems can rnams kyi rang bzhin gnyis mi g.yo bar byas na gdod mya ngan kyang med byang chub kyang med do//mya ngan 
4b2(mdo sna tshogs): kyang med byang chub kyang med/na gdod yang dag par bden pa'i gnas su chud de/de ni byang chub yang dag pa yin no//khyod la sogs pa byang chub sems dpas sems can zhig bstan cing dad na yang sems 
4b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'dug par ston cig/byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha' mdzod kyis sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das sems can bslab cing drang na de'i sems 'dug par bgyi na/khams gang la 'dug 
4b4(mdo sna tshogs): na byang chub thob par rung /sangs rgyas kyis byang chub sems dpa' nam 'kha' mdzod la bka' stsal pa/shin tu dge'o shin tu dge'o//rigs kyi bu pho khyod de 'dri re mkhas sems kyi phyi mo 'di lta bu 'dri ba 
4b5(mdo sna tshogs): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po yin te bsam gyis mi khyab bo//khyed rtags par nyon cig dang /khyed la zhib du bshad do//rigs kyi bu pho sems can bslabs shing drangs te sems 
4b6(mdo sna tshogs): 'dug par byin 'dug pa ni nang na yang mi 'dug/phyi rol na yang mi 'dug/bar na yang mi 'dug go/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi theg pa chen po'i chos kyang nang na yang mi 'dug/phyi rol na yang mi 'dugbar na yang mi 'dug go//
4b7(mdo sna tshogs): sems kyi shes pa gcig pu la gnas te/med pa'i gnas la 'dug pas na byang chub thob bo//byang chub thob tu zin kyang byang chub thob pa yod med de/de'i ming ni yang dag pa'i mi g.yo ba zhes bya'o//ji lta zhes byas na/
4b8(mdo sna tshogs): mya ngan ni mi bden pa las skyes te/mya ngan kyang stong pa'o//chos so cog kyang rang skyes pa med de chos kyang stong pa'o//sems shes bya ba yang gyin de ming yod du zad kyi thob par mi rung ste/sems kyang stong pa'o//rigs kyi bu pho 
5a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//
5b1(mdo sna tshogs): chos spyod pa rnams chos de bzhin gang bar shes na/gzhan gyi yul khams thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi lus btsal ba'i mi rigs kyi/sems gcig dang /khams gcig gi gnas la sangs rgyas kyi lus btsal pa'i 
5b2(mdo sna tshogs): rigs so//lus gcig gi nang nas/thams cad kyi lus 'byung bar nus so//thams cad kyi lus kyi nang nas lus gcig 'byung bar nus so//ji lta zhes byas na/lus gcig ni sangs rgyas gcig gi lus yin 
5b3(mdo sna tshogs): no//lus mang po thams cad ni sems can gyi lus yin pas na/kun sems gcig las skyes so//sems gcig pu dge na chos so cog zad par dge'o//sems gcig phyi mo mi dge na chos so cog zad bar yang mi 
5b4(mdo sna tshogs): dge'o//las ngan pa byas na sems can ngan pa'i lus su skye'o//las dge ba byas na lha dang mi'i lus su skye'o//sems gcig po yang sems gcig gi gzugs dang bral na sems kyi gzugs kyi nang na stong pa ste/
5b5(mdo sna tshogs): ci la'ang thob pa med do//stong pa'i khams dang yang bral na blang ba med pa'i sa ni sangs rgyas kyi lus skye nus so//sangs rgyas kyi lus la ni lus gcig tu zad kyi gnyis su med do//yod sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid ni gcig go/
5b6(mdo sna tshogs): sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid gcig gi nang na sems kyi ngo bo nyid gcig tu zad do//sems gcig po ma gtogs par gung du gzhan la btsal ba med do//gzhan la brtsal ba byas na de ni yon po mi drang ba'o//ji lta zhes 
5b7(mdo sna tshogs): byas na sems kyi chos thams cad ni phyi logs nas brtsal ba med do//phyi logs kyi gnas nas btsal na de'i ming ni gzhan bya'o//gzhan gyi gnas las btsal na de ni mi bden pa'o//byang chub sems dpa' 
5b8(mdo sna tshogs): nam mkha' mdzod kyis sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das sems gcig gi nang nas sangs rgyas thob par rung na/thams cad kyi sems kyi nang na/kun la yang sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid mchis pa 
6a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//lta sems can thams cad kun la sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid mchis na/yang dag par gshegs pa thams cad kyis sems gcig po spyod de/sems can thams cad bskyed nus te sems gcig 
6a2(mdo sna tshogs): lus gcig gis 'khrug pa bsal na/sangs rgyas kyi chos gzhan ma mchis te/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi sku lus su skyes lags pa lta/sangs rgyas kyis byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha' mdzod la bka' stsal 
6a3(mdo sna tshogs): pa/rigs kyi bu de yin no/de yin no//khyod kyis bshad pa bzhin te/sems can kun la yang sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid yod de/sangs rgyas yang dag par gshegs pa thas cad kun gyis sems gcig bu'i 
6a4(mdo sna tshogs): chos spyad de/sems can thams can kyi sems so cog sems gcig bu'i nang du sangs rgyas kyi chos tshol du btsud pas sangs rgyas kyi lus thob po//byang chub sems dpa' namamakha' mdzod kyis sangs rgyas 
6a5(mdo sna tshogs): la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid mchis te/sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid kyi nang du btsal bas sangs rgyas thob na sems can thams cad kyi so so'i sems kyi nang na yang 
6a6(mdo sna tshogs): sangs rgyas lags te/de thob par yang rung /mthong bar yang rung lags pa lta/sangs rgyas gyis byang chub sems dpa' mkha' mdzod la bka' stsalapa/rigs kyi bu pho sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid yang 
6a7(mdo sna tshogs): dag pa'i gzugs dam par 'jug pa ni thob ces byar yang mi rung ngo//mi thob ces byar yang mi rung/mthong zhes byar yang mi rung /mi mthong zhes byar yang mi rung/ji lta zhes byas na/byang chub sems 
6a8(mdo sna tshogs): dpas sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid yod ces bshad na/de'i ming ni sangs rgyas la bskur to//sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid med ces bshad na yang sangs rgyas la skur to//sangs rgyas kyi ngob nyid yod pa ma yin zhes 
6b1(mdo sna tshogs): bshad na yang sangs rgyas la bskur to//sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid med ces bshad na yang sangs rgyas las skur to//ji lta zhes byas na/byang chub sems dpa' sems can gyi sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid yod pa 
6b2(mdo sna tshogs): ma yin na ni nam mkha' dang mtshungs/med pa ma yin na ni ri bong gi rwa dang mtshungs te ri bong gi rwa ni yod med do//nam mkha' ni rtag par yod do//gzugs kyi tshul ni yod pa ma yin no//nam mkha'i tshul ni med pa 
6b3(mdo sna tshogs): ma yin no//gzugs kyi tshul kun dang bral na ci la'ang chags pa med do//skye ba'i gnas la mi 'dug ste/de la skye bar mthong na dri ma'o//med pa'i gnas na mi 'dug ste/de la ma mthong na chad pa zhes bya'o//sems 
6b4(mdo sna tshogs): can gyi sangs rgyas kyi ngo bonyid kyi dam pa'i gzugs ni de lta bu yin no//sangs rgyas kyi ngo bonyid yod ces bshad na bskyed de bskur to//sangs rgyas gyi ngo bo nyid med ces bshad/na smad de skur to//sangs rgyas kyi 
6b5(mdo sna tshogs): ngo bo nyid yod kyang yod/med kyang med ces bshad na log par skur to//sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid yod pa'ang ma yin med pa'ang ma yin zhes bshad na 'gran dor du skur to//byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha' 
6b6(mdo sna tshogs): mdzod kyis sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das sems can gyi sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid dam pa de bzhin du zab ste/'du mched kyi rnams dang bral bas cis kyang gos pa ma mchis te/dper na nam 
6b7(mdo sna tshogs): mkha' dang mtshungs shing phyi mo'i ngo bo nyid rtag par gtsang na ci'i sled nas sems can gyis mya ngan rnams bgyis/mya ngan bgyis na sems nyid dri ma lags pa lta na/sems kyi dri ma de thog ma ga las skyes pa 
6b8(mdo sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das kyis phal mang po la bshad de/sems can gyi rnams kun gyis thos shing shes par bgyis te/sems can gyi rnams kyi mya ngan ldan no 'tshal bshig ste/sems kyi dri ma bkrus bsal 
7a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//la bkab cing bkris pa yong bral bar smon to//de nas sangs rgyas 'dzum mul zhes bzhad de phyag g.yon pas byang chub sems dpa'i spyi bo la phyis te/'od chen po btang nas thams cad kun tu 
7a2(mdo sna tshogs): khyab par byas so//de nas sems can phal mang po thams cad kun shin du dga' gzha'o//sangs rgyas kyi zhal nas byang chub sems dpas sems can rnams kyi phyir de lta bu'i dri ba byas pa de'i ming ni drang po dris 
7a3(mdo sna tshogs): pa ste/sems can thar par bya ba yin no//tshig gzhan dris na de'i ming ni yon por dris shes bya ste/sems can brlag pa yin no//byang chub sems dpa' khyod drang po 'dri nus pa sems can thar par byas pa yin no//
7a4(mdo sna tshogs): khyed la sogs pa sems can kun sems gcig tu sems la brtags par nyon te/'gyes shing 'khrug pa'i sems sol la phyi logs kyi don med par byos te/sems kyi yid thub par 'dug shig dang khyed la 
7a5(mdo sna tshogs): bshad do//bshad pa thos na ngan pa bcu'i sems can kun grol thar pa thob bo//byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha' mdzod kyis sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das bdag la sogs pa sems can 
7a6(mdo sna tshogs): phal mang po thams cad kun sems gcig tu bgyis lags te/'khrug pa'i sems gzhan ma mchis na/bcom ldan 'das kyis phal mang po la dgrol bshad par smon to//bcom ldan 'das kyis byang 
7a7(mdo sna tshogs): chub sems dpa' dang phal mang po rnams la bka' stsal pa/mya ngan thams cad ni phyin ci log las skyes so//phyin ci log thams cad ni mi bde na pa'i sems las skyes so//mi bden pa'i sems thams 
7a8(mdo sna tshogs): cad ni nga yod pa las skyes so//nga yod pa thams cad ni phyi mo yod med pa las skyes so//phyi mo med pa thams cad ni mi 'dug pa nyid yin no//phyi mo med na yod pa ma yin no//yod pa ni dri ma'o//med pa ni gtsang ma'o//
7b1(mdo sna tshogs): gtsang ma de'i gnas ni rtag par pha rol tu phyin pa yin no//skyid pa pha rol tu phyin pa yin na no//nga pha rol tu phyin pa yin te/gtsang ma pha rol tu phyin pa yin pa ni/de lta bu mthong ba ni ming drang po mthong ba zhes bya ste/gzhan 
7b2(mdo sna tshogs): mthong ba ni ming yin pa mthong ba zhes bya'o//de lta bu mthong ba ni mi de la shes rab yod do//gzhan mthong ba ni mi de la shes/rab med do//shes rab yod pas ni thabs kyis dkrol lo//shes rab med pas ni thabs kyis 
7b3(mdo sna tshogs): bcings so//byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha' mdzod kyis sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das gtsang ma'i gnas de na sems can la la zhig sems rtag tu rtag pa'i sems g.yos so//rtag 
7b4(mdo sna tshogs): tu skyid pa'i sems g.yos so//rtug tu nga'i sems g.yo'o//rtag tu gtsang ma'i sems g.yo'o//shes rab yod pa lags te phyin ci log ma lags so//sangs rgyas kyis byang chub sems dpa' nam 'kha' mdzod 
7b5(mdo sna tshogs): la bka' stsal pa/sems can la la zhig de ltar sems pa ni ming drang po sems pa ste/mi de drang po mthong ba yin no//mi de shes rab yod pa yin no//ji lta zhes byas na/yang dag par gshegs pa'i chos kyi sku 
7b6(mdo sna tshogs): lus rtag pa pha rol tu phyin/skyid pa pha rol tu phyin/de pha rol tu phyin/gtsang ma pha rol tu phyin te gtsang gdang bar gnas so//sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos kyi sku lus de lta bur mthong ba'i ming de ni sangs rgyas kyi 
7b7(mdo sna tshogs): yang dag pa'i slob ma gcig pu yin te/drang po'i chos las skyes chos las 'phrul te skyes/sangs rgyas kyi zhal nas skyes/sangs rgyas kyi bstan pa bzhi thob bo//mi tha mal pa yin mod kyi brtan pa bzhi byang chub sems 
7b8(mdo sna tshogs): dpa' yin no//rigs kyi bu pho nga 'das pa'i phyin lo lnga brgya yang rung /lo stong yang rung /lo stong lnga brgya yang rung /de'i phyi ni yang mi la la zhig gis chos kyi yi ge 'di gzungs su gzung ste/kha ton dang klog nus la bshad 
8a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//pa bzhin du las su byas te/rtag skyid dang gtsang gi gnas la yid ches shing drang por mthong gi steng du chos 'di spyad de/sems can bstan bslabs na/sems can gcig bstan na de'i ming byang chub 
8a2(mdo sna tshogs): sems dpa'o//mi tha mal pa mod kyi yon phul ba blang du rung ste/khyim nas byung ba'i mi yin no//byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha' mdzod kyis sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das yod khyim nas byung ba'i 
8a3(mdo sna tshogs): ming de skra dang kha spu bregs la chos kyi gos gyon te/tshul khrims tshad pa mnos te/tha mal pa'i chos kyis ma gos pa de'i ming ni khyim nas byung ste/yon blang du rung ba zhes bgyi na/yang dag par gshegs pas nga 
8a4(mdo sna tshogs): bshad pa mi tha mal pa la khyim nas byung ba'i mi lags te/yon blang du rung zhes bgyi ba de'i gzhung bye brag ma phyed na/sangs rgyas kyis byams shing thugs rje gzigs te bdag la bshad par smon to//sangs rgyas kyis 
8a5(mdo sna tshogs): byang chub sems dpa' namamakha'i mdzod la bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu pho skra dang kha spu bregs pa ni lus la da med par gdul ba'i phyir ro//chos kyi gos gyon pa ni tha mal pa'i chos dang dbral ba'i phyir ro//tshul khrims 
8a6(mdo sna tshogs): tshad par 'dzin pa ni thub cing mi gzhig pa'i phyir ro//ngas bshad pa de mi tha mal pa yin mod kyi lus dang sems thul bar nus te/nga dang le lo mi g.yo sems la rdul kyis ma gos te/tha mal pa'i chos dang bral nas 
8a7(mdo sna tshogs): yun rings te/rdo rje dang mtshungs te/tshul khrims kyi ngo bo nyid ma zhig pas/tha mal pa yin mod kyi yang dag par na khyim nas byung ba yang dag pa yin no//yang theg pa chen po'i chos kyi yi ge 'di'i nang na bshad pa 
8a8(mdo sna tshogs): bzhin du las su spyad na/sangs rgyas kyi chos la yid ches pas rtag skyid nga gtsang mthong ste/sems can la dkrol bshad na mi tha mal pa yin mod kyis brtan pa bzhi'i mi yin te/byang chub sems dpa'i las 
8b1(mdo sna tshogs): spyod pa yin blang du rung ste ming ni dge bsnyen zhes bya'o//shes rab kyi thabs thob ste theg pa chen po'i chos bshad do//de lta bu'i chos kyi ngo bo nyid kun sems dang ma bral te/sems las 'phrul skyes te ting nge 'dzin 
8b2(mdo sna tshogs): mi g.yo rtag par gcig pu gcig pu'i sems la gnyis su med do//gcig pu'i sems kyi nang na phyi nang yang med/bar ma yang med de thams cad dang bral lo//thams cad dang bral na skye ba dang shi ba'ang med do//skye 
8b3(mdo sna tshogs): ba dang shi ba med na gdod yang dag pa'i mi g.yo ba yin no//yang dag pa'i mi g.yo ba rtag par 'dug la chos dang dge 'dun ni med par mi 'gyur ro//khams gsum gyi sems can ni rang skyes rang shi'o//rigs kyi bu pho 
8b4(mdo sna tshogs): ched kar sems can lang med par bshad do//sangs rgyas dang yang dag par gshegs pa ni yang dag pa'i bden pa'ang yin te/skye shir 'bab pa las 'das so//khyod la sogs pa sems can rnams rtag par 
8b5(mdo sna tshogs): 'dug pa tshol na 'dod pa'i rnams dang spongs la skye ba med pa'i las byos shig/byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha' mdzod kyi sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das las ji lta bu spyad na ming skye ba med pa'i las 
8b6(mdo sna tshogs): shes bgyi/sangs rgyas kyi zhal nas/rigs kyi bu pho rnams nga las gzhan ma skyes na/gzhan las nga mi skye'o//ji lta zhes byas na/tshul gyi ngo bo nyid ni gtsang ma stong pa ste/yod pa'ang ma yin/rtag pa med 
8b7(mdo sna tshogs): pa yang ma yin no//yod do cog kyang yod ni stong pa gtsang ma ste/med pa yang ma yin na mthar yang yang ba yang ma yin no//byang chub sems dpa' dang sangs rgyas yang dag par gshegs pa rnams kyis 'khor 
8b8(mdo sna tshogs): lor ngo bo nyid gcig gi spos kyi ro bro bas sems can rnams kyi sems gcig gi ngo bo nyid gtsang mar bsangs na/'dzangs pa rnams ni go ste stong par khong du chud/blun po'i rnams kyis ni bslad pa'i nang du

9b1(mdo sna tshogs): nus/sangs rgyas kyi zhal nas byang chub sems dpas las spyod pa'i rnams la sa khor mor sems 'dug pa'i rnams la rtog tu bcug ste/sems 'dug pa'i gnas rig na gdod sems mi 'dug go/de ltar 
9b2(mdo sna tshogs): tshor bas na tshor ba dgag go/'dod pa'i sems mi g.yo na dgag cing brtag pa yang med do//g.yo ba'i gnas yod na stong pa la bde bar bzhag go/ngo bo nyid kyi stong pa gcig gi nang na gos pa dang gtsang ma gnyis ka 
9b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'ang med do//ngo bo nyid gtsang gdang bas na blangs pa 'ang med gtad pa 'ang med do//sems kyang mi skye'o//skye ba 'ang med/spyod pa 'ang med/lus kyis spyad pa'i gnas kyang med/tshig smra ba'i lam yang chad/sangs rgyas kyi 
9b4(mdo sna tshogs): ngo bo nyid kyi ngo bo nyid kyi dam pa tshor ba'i ngo bo nyid ni rtag par yod cing /dpe stong pa dang mtshungs te thams cad la ma chags so//byang chub sems dpas las 'di spyad na/dper dbyi gu thogs la nam mkha' la rdeg pa dang 
9b5(mdo sna tshogs): tshungs ste bog ni phog ste chags pa med do//de'i ming chos spyod pa zhes bya'o//chags pa'i gnas yod du zin na phyi nang du zhugs pa yin no//sa gtsang mar bgrod cing 'gro na sems gcig ste gnyis su med/bsam 
9b6(mdo sna tshogs): gtan du zhugs la ldang dub kyi sems bkag ste/yang dag pa'i bden pa gcig la brtags la 'gyod tshangs bya'o//de'i tshe phal mang po'i nang na log sred mi 'dod mangs shes bya ba zhig yod pa gnas thog ma med 
9b7(mdo sna tshogs): pa tshun chad log pa mang du byas te/nyes pa bcu rtag par byas nas ldang ba dang /khro ba dang /phrag dog dang /'gran zla ba dang /'di rnams bzhi'i sbrul gyis ni drangs/mi bden pa dang phyi log gi sems kyi byi ba 
9b8(mdo sna tshogs): gnyis kyis ni sems kyi rtsa ba bcad de/dper na mi zhig shing gi kha na mar sbyangs la 'og na sprul bzhi 'dug ste/kha gdug pa'i blangs pas ni gyen du gdugs/shing gi kha nas byi ba gnyis kyis ni dpyad thag 
10a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//gi rtsa ba la bzas te chad la thug/sems gtsang ma gsum ni med par byas/mig dang kha dog dang /sems dang kun ni mi bden pa phyin ci log byed pa ste/mthong bas bcings nas sems can dmyal 
10a2(mdo sna tshogs): bar lhung la thug pas las skad cig bsam pa'i sems kyi nang na gnod bkur ba'i sems g.yos te/yang dag par gshegs pa la 'gyod tshangs kyi chos 'dri 'am snyam na yid la gnod zhing ngo tsha bas 'dri ma phod na/yang 
10a3(mdo sna tshogs): dag par gshegs pa'i 'phrul gyi thugs kyis de'i yid la sems dpA mkhyen te/mi de sdug bsngal ba las bral te/sems can dmyal ba'i sgo nas dbyung bar thugs la dgongs nas byang chub sems dpa' 
10a4(mdo sna tshogs): nam mkha' mdzod la bka' stsal pa/nga 'das te song ba'i phyin log srid la la zhig gis las ngan pa mang du byas te/sangs rgyas kyi dkon mchog gsum med par byed pa de lta bu'i mi ni phyi ma'i dus la sems can 
10a5(mdo sna tshogs): dmyal ba'i nang du yang dag par lhung nas bskal pa chen po bcu gnyis kyi bar du'ang mi thar to//khyed byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis byams shing snying rje spyod la sems can 'di lta bu'i rnams 'gyod tshangs byed du 
10a6(mdo sna tshogs): chug la/de'i sems kyi dri ma gtsang gdang bar byos la grol thar par byos shig/byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha' mdzod kyis sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das 'gyod tshangs kyi chos ci zhig bgyis 
10a7(mdo sna tshogs): na/sdig btsal bar rung ba sangs rgyas kyis byams shing thugs rje gzigs te/bye brag phyed par bshad par smon to//sangs rgyas kyis byang chub sems dpa' namamakha' mdzod la bka' stsal pa/'gyod 
10a8(mdo sna tshogs): tshangs bya na yang dag pa'i bden pa'i gzhud la brtag go/yang dag pa'i bden pa'i bzhud mthong na sdig go cog thams cad bsal to//sangs rgyas kyis tshig 'di skad bshad zin nas de'i tshe pal mang po brgya stong 
10b1(mdo sna tshogs): khri 'bum gyi sems can mi dang mi ma yin pa la sogs pa thams cad kun sems cig tu yang dag pa'i bden pa'i gzhung gcig la brtags te/mthong zin nas log sred 'dod mangs mod bzhin bstan las langs te 
10b2(mdo sna tshogs): lus smad lnga sa la gtugs nas sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das bdag gi sems la ma rig pa mchis pas brtag pa bslabs mod kyi yang dag pa'i bden pa'i bzhud ma mthong ngo //thog ma zhes 
10b3(mdo sna tshogs): bgyi ba ma mchis pa tshun chad ngan pa bcu 'ba' shig gi las bgyis na/nga thabs ci spyad na bdag gis yang dag pa'i bden pa'i mthun mthong zhing sdig zhu byang bar bgyir rung /sangs rgyas kyi 'dod mangs la bka' stsal 
10b4(mdo sna tshogs): pa/khyod la sogs pa log sred kyi rnams kun sems gcig tu byos shig dang /ngas khyed la bye brag phyed par dgrol bshad do//'dod mangs khyod la sogs pa sems can gyi rnams kyis lus dang sems la 
10b5(mdo sna tshogs): sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo gcig bu la brtags na chos kyi ngo bo gcig nyid kyang brtag pa gcig bu'o//sangs rgyas so cog la'ang ngo bo nyid gcig bu ste/gnyis su med pas na yang dag pa'i bden pa'i gzhung yin 
10b6(mdo sna tshogs): no//de la gnyis su yod par mthong na de'i ming ni yon po zhes bya'o//yon po la lta na ni mya ngan 'khrug pa ldang ngo //yon po mthong ba med na mya ngan 'khrug pa'ang mi skye'o//mya ngan 'khrug pa chod na de'i ming 
10b7(mdo sna tshogs): gtsang ga dang zhes bya'o//khyed la sogs pa sems can kun gyis sems gcig tu sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid gcig bu la rtogs shig/sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid las gzhan mthong ba ni yod med do//gzhan 
10b8(mdo sna tshogs): mthong ba yod du zin na kun kyang sgyu ma mi bden pa yin no//mi bden pa de lta bu byed pa ni phyin ci log ces bya'o//'dod mangs sems gcig gtsang na ni chos so cog kun kyang gtsang ngo //sems gcig smon

11b1(mdo sna tshogs): 'dral ba yin no//chos la zhig cing ral na spyod pa la'ang rtag pa med de/chos la skye shi yod par 'gyur ro//skye shi yod pa'i chos dang bral na gdod ming yang dag pa'i gzhung zhes bya'o//'dod mangs dper na 
11b2(mdo sna tshogs): rdza mkhan gyis rdza spyad byas pa dang mtshungs te/yid la ci 'dod pa bzhin du byas na spyad la mi 'gyur ba med de/spyad kyi dpe gzugs dang ming kun skyes shing dengs so//gyo mo'i ring bzhin 'ba' shig 
11b3(mdo sna tshogs): yod de/rang bzhin gcig pu ni rtag par yod do//dpe gzugs gzhan kun ni skyes shing dengs so//skyes shing dengs pa'i gzugs ni rang skyes pa ma yin no//rang skyes pa ma yin na yod pa ma yin no//
11b4(mdo sna tshogs): 'dod mangs sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid ni dper gyo mo dang mtshungs so//sems can gyi rang bzhin ni dper snod dang mtshungs te skyes shi'i chos yin no//las so cog dang bral na sangs rgyas kyi sku lus gcig 
11b5(mdo sna tshogs): yin te/sangs rgyas kyi sku lus gcigs la brtags na lus gzhan med do//'dod mangs kyi sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das bdag gis yang dag pa'i bden pa'i gzhung la brtags pas sdig 
11b6(mdo sna tshogs): gi rnam kun byang lags na yang las ci spyad na chos kyi lus skye bar rung /sangs rgyas kyi 'dod mangs la bka' stsal pa/yang dag pa'i bden pa'i bzhud la brtags na nad do cog mi ldang ngo //sdig dang dri ma kun 
11b7(mdo sna tshogs): ma g.yos na sems ni rdo rje dang mtshungs te nam du'ang mi 'jig go/tshul khrims gzung nus na sems nam mkha' dang mtshungs te/phyi nang gnyis ka gtsang gdang ngo //bsam gtan 'dus pa'i nang du 'jug pa 
11b8(mdo sna tshogs): la mkhas na/sems ni dper rlung dang me dang mtshungs te/las ngan pa'o cog kun gyis shing bsal to//shes rab la rten pa la mkhas na shes rab thob par rung ste/de'i ming ni grol thar pa zhes bya'o//
12a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//grol thar pas na yang dag pa shes shing mthong bar nus so//'dod mangs khyod kyis 'di lta bu'i don sbyang zhing sbyad nus na chos kyi sku lus rnam pa lnga thob par rung ngo//'dod mangs kyis sangs rgyas la 
12a2(mdo sna tshogs): gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das chos kyi lus rnam lnga la rgyu dang 'bras bu ci mchis/sangs rgyas kyi 'dod mangs la bka' stsal pa/sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid ni rtag pa'i rgyu yin no//chos kyi sku lus ni rtag pa'i 'bras bu 
12a3(mdo sna tshogs): yin no//ji lta zhes byas na/sems kyi rgyu ni sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid/de chos kyi yon gyis 'bras bu thob po//rgyu dang 'bras bu dang bral na rgyu'i 'bras bu med do//rgyu dang 'bras bu med na sangs rgyas kyi lus yang dag pa 
12a4(mdo sna tshogs): yin no//'dod mangs sems gtsang ma'i lus la sems mi g.yo bar sangs rgyas tshe dpag med pa sems na gdod yul gtsang mar skye ste/sems can gyi phyir mi g.yo ba'i chos bshad de/sems yang dag pa'i stong 
12a5(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i nang du chud la g.yo zhing bshad pa'i rnams dang bral te/shes pa gsum gyi ngo bo nyid gcig pa/rdo rje dang mtshungs par mi 'jig go/de'i tshe phal gyi nang na yang byang chub sems dpa' ming spyod med ces bya ba zhig yod pa 
12a6(mdo sna tshogs): stan las langs te/phrag pa g.yas pa chos bzhin byas/pus mo g.yas pa sa la gtsugs te thal mo sbyar nas sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das sangs rgyas 'das te gshegs pa'i slad na lo lnga brgya 
12a7(mdo sna tshogs): 'am/lo stong ngam/stong lnga brgya na sems can la chos bshad na/chos gang bshad pa'i rigs pa sangs rgyas kyis byams shing thugs rje gzigs te bdag la drang zhing bshad par smon to//sangs rgyas kyis byang chub sems 
12a8(mdo sna tshogs): dpa' spyod med la bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu pho chos kyis rnams bshad pa ni mdo ni bshad gzugs ni mi bshad do//gzhung ni bshad/yi ge'i ri mo ni mi bshad do//de lta bur bshad pa ni chos yang dag pa yin no//
12b1(mdo sna tshogs): yi ge'i ri mo dang /gzugs bshad na ni kha g.yos sems g.yos pa kun kyang bkur cing /dbang bzas pa'i rigs kyi bu po sems can thams cad la chos bshad na mi g.yo ba bzhin du bshad/mnyam snyoms 
12b2(mdo sna tshogs): par bshad pa'i rigs so//ji lta zhes byas na/sems can no cog gi sems gcig la sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid kyang gcig ste/ngo bo nyid gcig tu mnyam snyoms na chos so cog kun kyang mnyam snyoms so//de la 
12b3(mdo sna tshogs): mthon dman du bshad na de'i ming yin po bshad pa ste/de'i kha ni ral/de'i lce ni gas te/ci'i phyir zhes byas na/sems can thams cad kyi sems kyi dri ma yang dri ma gcig tu 'thun no//sems can thams cad kyi 
12b4(mdo sna tshogs): sems gtsang ma yang gtsang ma gcig tu 'thun na/ci'i phyir shes kyis na/sems can thams can kyi sems gcig tu gtsang ste/dge ba bcu'i chos 'thun par gtsang ngo //sems can thams cad gyi sems gcig 
12b5(mdo sna tshogs): du dri ma yod na ngan pa bcu'i chos gcig tu 'thun par dri ma yod do//sems can gyi nad ni gcig tu 'thun te/sems can gyi sman dgos na sman kyang gcig tu dgos so//chos mang du bshad na de'i ming ni phyin ci log 
12b6(mdo sna tshogs): ces bya'o//ci'i phyir zhes byas na/mi bden pas bye brag phye ste/dge ba dang ngan pa'i chos bshig ste/chos gcig bshig cing yod bzhin du chos bshad na/sangs rgyas kyi chos lam bshad pa yin no//byang 
12b7(mdo sna tshogs): chub sems dpas chos bshad pa na mi g.yo ba bzhin du shod cig/byang chub sems dpa' spyod med kyis sangs rgyas la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das ji ltar mi g.yo ba bzhin du bshad ces bgyi/sangs rgyas 
12b8(mdo sna tshogs): kyis byang chub sems dpa' spyod maid la bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu pho yang dag pa'i bden pa'i chos gcig bshad pa ni mi g.yo ba bzhin du bshadapa yin no//sems drang po rdzogs par bshad pa ni mi g.yo

13b1(mdo sna tshogs): de lta bu'i chos rnams rdzogs nang ni sems gcig gi na sangs rgyas ngo bo nyid gcig gi sar gtsang ma gcig snyoms par bshad na/de'i ming rdzogs par bshad na de mi g.yo ba bzhin bshad ces bya'o//ci'i phyi

13b4(mdo sna tshogs): snying gtsang ma gcig tu snyoms na sems gcig tu gtsang /sangs rgyas kyi ngo bo nyid gcig tu gtsang /chos kyi rnams gcig tu gtsang ste/mnyam snyoms na chos kyi rnams snyoms te/de lta bur bshad pa ni gtsang

13b6(mdo sna tshogs): yod du zad do//rtsa ba'i phyi mo bzhin du theg pa chung ngu'i mi la ni theg pa chung ngu'i chos bshad/log sred kyi mi la ni log sred kyi chos bshad na de'i ming ni sangs rgyas kyi chos la ma yon por bshad pa zhes bya ste/sangs rgyas kyi

13b8(mdo sna tshogs): mi thar to//thar du zin te mi'i nang du skyes na yang yul gyi mtha' 'khob mi'i tha ma'i nang du skyes te/mchu sho re la lce med pa de lta bu'i lus thob bo//ci'i phyi nas shes byas pa/byang chub sems dpa' sems can

14b3(mdo sna tshogs): zhing sgral ba'i chos nyung du thos la mang du shes pa dang /nyung du mthong ba la mang du rig pa dang /yi ge'i ri mo mang du ma btsal bar gzhung gi mngon rtags thob pa yang bshad kyis/khyed la sogs pa byang chub sems
